As we delve into the realm of episodic storytelling, we recognize that we stand on the shoulders of giants – those who have dedicated their lives to shaping minds, empowering hearts, and crafting narratives that transcend the boundaries of ordinary influence. Their collective wisdom serves as an anchor, grounding us in the principles of equality, authenticity, and transformation. The people you will hear from next are some of the best international hypnotists, performers, and experts in their respective fields. I am extremely grateful to have them as colleagues, friends, and subject matter experts that can handle any question I can think up.

As readers embark on this journey of exploration and self-expression, I am reminded of the common ground that unites us all. The power of hypnotic storytelling, when wielded with compassion and purpose, becomes a force for positive change in our lives and the lives of others. We extend our deepest gratitude to those who have shared their wisdom in this endeavor. Together, we can harness the art of episodic storytelling to create a better world, one narrative at a time. This moment marks the start of another story that you begin to carry forward into the future. Thank you for embarking on this transformative quest with us.





Foreword Dr. Jonathan Royle


“VERY SILENTLY I CREPT FORWARD, I HEARD A SNARLING GRUNTING SOUND FROM ABOVE ME, NOW I LOOKED UPWARDS, THERE WAS THE TIGER, ITS MOUTH OPEN WIDE SHOWING LARGE TEARING FANGS, THE BLACK PATCHES ON THE ROOF OF ITS MOUTH STOOD OUT WITH STARTLING CLARITY, WITHOUT ANOTHER SOUND, IT LEAPT DOWN TOWARDS ME, I CROUCHED LOWER, AND IT PASSED OVER ME, BEFORE THE TIGER COULD TURN, I LEAPT ONTO ITS BACK, CIRCLED ITS THROAT WITH MY LEFT ARM, AND RAISED MY RIGHT ARM WITH THE LONG DAGGER IN IT, VERY HIGH, READY TO STRIKE INTO THE TIGERS JUGULAR.”



That paragraph held your attention, that is what a Story Teller or as they can also be called a Hypnotist does, they grab your attention then hypnotize you.



My name is Dr. Jonathan Royle and I have been a Professional Hypnotherapist Since 1989 & Professional Comedy Stage Hypnotist since 1990 and as is illustrated at www.MagicalGuru.co.uk I am now regarded by many as one of the World’s Leading Teachers & Trainers of all things relating to Hypnosis, NLP, Mind Control, Psychological Persuasion & Influence.



More recently it has been my pleasure and honour for two years in a row to be awarded “Agent of Influence” Awards by Joseph Crown as well as being invited to become a Licensed Trainer of his powerful life changing “Constellation Experience” approach & workshops.



I have also been fortunate enough to get to know Joseph Crown and to share Hypnotic Approaches & Insights with each other as he is now also a member of my “Elite Hypnosis Bootcamp” and I have interviewed Master Crown for my Hypnosis Week Podcast which you can enjoy here – https://podcasters.spotify.com/pod/show/jonathan-royle and can also watch the video interview versions of via my CelebrityHypnotist You-Tube Channel Hypnosis Week Playlist here

Having read this amazing new blue print to Story Telling Success which is meticulously detailed, highly educational and yet entertaining & emotionally stimulating in equal measure I have absolutely no hesitations in unreservedly recommending that every student of Hypnosis, Persuasion, Influence and related areas would be wise to secure a copy of this book for their own personal study.



Indeed I can see that this book will (or at least should) also find a market and great interest among Script Writers of Stage & Screen Plays as well as those focused on writing their own works of fiction or indeed reality.



And need I point out that stimulating emotional responses and imparting a powerful attention grabbing story are the keys to being a truly effective Sales Person & Influential Manager of People both in the work place and also in your personal life such as in the dating arena.



With that in mind, within these pages you are being offered the Keys to unlock the pathways to success in all areas of your life.



Your quest should you choose to accept it is to pay attention as if your life depended on it, stay focused on every little detail and just allow everything you read and observe to be absorbed into every nerve, fiber, tissue and muscle in your body from the tips of your toes to the tips of your fingers and everywhere in between as you positively start to transform into the powerful & persuasive hypnotic story teller that nature always intended you to be.



ABRACADABRA is a Magic Word which is derived from the Aramaic phrase avra kehdabra, meaning “I will create as I speak” and it is indeed true that words used effectively create mental images, stimulate emotional responses and can arguably be the verbal “magic wand” that triggers positive realizations, harmonious life changes and even profound healing on all levels in a person.



Within the pages that follow you will discover the art of using words to attract such magical responses, the art of painting vivid bright (and sometimes where required dark) pictures with your words and above all the art of being truly Hypnotic in all you say whether out-loud verbally or in print.

Foreword by Master Damien Atropa


It was a Saturday, sometime in the mid-2010s. Master Crown and I were sharing drinks at a bar we frequent. Whenever we visited this bar, we kept an empty seat between us to welcome the occasional weary traveler. And soon after midnight, that’s exactly what happened. A gentleman walked in, wearing a tie that had visibly loosened after a long day at work, with eyes that looked as if they hadn’t faced sleep in weeks.

This gentleman sat between us, and Master Crowne ordered him a whiskey. One thing you learn from spending time with Master Crown, is that he has a magnetic charisma. I’ve never known anyone to share 30 minutes with him, and not treat him like a newfound confidante. And that’s exactly what happened.

Soon, the gentleman found himself entrusting Master Crown with his deepest struggles, baring his soul about the challenges at work with his employer, the haunting fears lurking in his personal life, and the haunting disappointment he carried within himself. He confided about the relentless battle he fought with substances and nicotine, seeking refuge from the overwhelming stress that life threw his way. And then, my friends, something truly unforgettable transpired. Master Crown's eyes sparkled with understanding as he gently patted the man on the back, and with a knowing smile, he uttered those magical words, "Let me tell you a story."

And as those words escaped his lips, a hushed silence fell upon the surrounding patrons, drawn in like moths to a mesmerizing flame. Ah, you see, this was the coveted spectacle at the bar during those hours. If fate favored you, you'd be privileged to witness Master Crown's enchanting storytelling, a show like no other. So esteemed were these storytelling sessions that bartenders in our cherished clubs would eagerly save us a spot in the cozy corner, for they knew that the allure of our performances was a boon for their business.

The gentleman, at first unsure of what awaited him, sat with a hint of curiosity as Master Crown began to weave his tale. Little did he know that he would be transported into a realm of captivating enchantment within moments. As the story unfolded, an invisible thread of fascination enveloped not just him but everyone around. The clinking of glasses became a mere echo, subdued by the magnetic pull of Master Crown's words.

Such is the profound allure of stories - they possess the sorcery to transport us into uncharted territories, where worries fade, and imaginations come alive. In those precious 20 minutes, it felt as if time had momentarily stood still, and we were spellbound, entangled in a web of wonder.

Master Crown's storytelling prowess is more than just an amusing diversion; it's a gateway to new worlds, where our defenses fall, and our hearts open to the inexplicable charm of the unknown. His tales hold the power to disarm apprehensions, inviting us to embrace the mystical and fantastical corners of our own souls.

In those moments, we are no longer mere spectators; we are voyagers, carried by the currents of magic that still flow within us, eager to explore the depths of our deepest desires and dreams. For when the words of a skilled storyteller like Master Crown touch our hearts, they rekindle that precious part of ourselves that believes in the enchantment of life.

So, as the echoes of his story fade into memory, we are left with an indelible truth - the power of storytelling is a force that connects us all, binding us together in the shared experience of wonder and imagination. And as long as there are tales to be told, that magical part of us will forever yearn to be whisked away on new adventures, believing in the ever-present allure of magic.

While Master Crown and I bid farewell to that gentleman that fateful night, life carried us forward, and as it often does, the winds of change swept us all in different directions. Years passed, and the memories of that enchanting encounter became a cherished tale to be shared among friends.

Then, one fine day, like a twist in a well-crafted plot, our favorite bartender received an unexpected call. A guest had graced the bar with a wide, warm smile and a heart full of treasured family photos. He regaled the bartender with stories that flowed like the finest elixir, and the minutes turned into hours as they delved into the realms of memory.

But here's the curious part – as the night drew to a close, this guest did something that set him apart. He left a generous tip to express his gratitude for the soul-stirring conversations shared in that hallowed space. And then, he made a request that tugged at the heartstrings of the bartender. He asked the bartender to pass on his heartfelt thanks to the storyteller who had spun tales of wonder years ago. That storyteller, whose words had touched his heart, left him searching for one thing, and he wanted them to know the profound impact they had on a wandering soul.

As the tale reached Master Crown's ears, he couldn't help but smile with a knowing glint in his eye. It was a reminder of the power of storytelling, the magic of connection, and the timeless influence of those who weave words with skill and heart. So, my dear readers, let this be a testament to the ripple effect of our stories. They traverse time and space, leaving imprints on hearts and minds we may never know. And who knows, perhaps one day, a stranger will raise their glass and offer a toast to the storyteller whose words touched their soul, just like that wide-smiled guest at the bar.

Stories have a special kind of magic. They can touch the human heart and mold cultures and civilizations for millennia. When combined with hypnotic storytelling techniques, these familiar concepts can capture listeners, elicit emotions, and enact permanent change. Master Crown is one of the foremost masters of the craft, and in this guide, he will lead you down a path where the lines between storyteller and audience become beautifully blurred. You will learn how to plant the seeds of ideas deep within the receptive minds of those who listen.

The Constellation Experience frameworks in this guide unlock the ability to trace hypnotic effects across short stories, novels, series, and a mesmerizing fusion of hypnotic curated adventures. This guide defines fundamental structures and techniques of hypnotic storytelling and imparts the wisdom and experience required to harness them. This guide takes you from fundamentals to advanced use cases quickly. Do the work and get the benefits!

As you embark on this quest to refine your storytelling prowess, know that your name may soon become a legend in the Great Game of hypnotic influence. The magic awaits, and your journey begins now.

Master Damien Atropa

Hypno Dom &amp; Fellow Hypnotic Storyteller





Foreword by Kelly Colombel


Crafting Eternal Narratives on the Canvas of the Mind and Body

In the discipline where the art of hypnosis intertwines with the mastery of tattooing, a realm where stories are etched upon the soul and symbols come to life on the body, Joseph Crown's work stands as a testament to the profound impact of both disciplines. As a fellow practitioner in the arts of hypnosis and a tattoo artist, I have had the privilege of witnessing firsthand the transformative power that stories and symbols hold over the human psyche. Now, in "The Art of Hypnotic Episodic Storytelling," Joseph Crown weaves these two worlds together, unveiling a mesmerizing tapestry of techniques and insights that bridges the gap between the mind and the body.

In this extraordinary manual, Crown expertly navigates the intricacies of constructing longer episodic stories in the realm of hypnosis, a feat that resonates deeply with my craft as a tattoo artist. We understand the potency of narratives, the way they can mold perceptions and emotions, and how they become imprinted upon our beings, both in mind and on flesh. Just as I craft permanent designs on the canvas of the skin, Crown artfully demonstrates how to create enduring stories that leave ineffaceable imprints on the canvas of the mind.

As readers embark on this literary journey, they will find themselves transported into a world of hypnotic storytelling, where the boundaries between imagination and reality blur, much like the line between art and the body. Crown's expertise guides us through the delicate process of world-building, helping us cultivate narratives that seep into the subconscious and influence the very fabric of our existence.

However, it is not only the mind that craves lasting symbols. Just as a tattoo carries profound meanings, Crown's narratives are imbued with powerful symbolism, yearning to manifest in tangible forms. The fusion of hypnotic stories and the sacred art of tattooing invites us to explore the union of the ethereal and the material, where the stories etched upon the soul seek embodiment in the symbols etched upon the body.

In "The Art of Hypnotic Episodic Storytelling," Joseph Crown shares a treasure trove of techniques and insights that transcend both the art of hypnosis and the mastery of tattooing. It is a book that beckons us to embrace the extraordinary potential of narrative and symbolism, offering us a glimpse into the artistry that shapes our minds and bodies. So, embark on this journey of exploration and creation, for within these pages, you will discover the secrets to crafting undying narratives that find expression not only in the mind but also upon the canvas of the body.

Foreword: Jim Kellner


Crafting compelling narratives is a timeless art that has woven its magic into the fabric of human existence for centuries. From ancient myths to contemporary literature, storytellers have left an indelible mark on cultures, beliefs, and societies. Hypnotism, with its profound ability to influence, finds a special place within this narrative landscape. In "The Art of Hypnotic Episodic Storytelling," Joseph Crown, a seasoned hypnotist with two decades of experience, embarks on a profound exploration of long-form hypnotic storytelling, enriching our understanding of episodic narratives and their immense power to shape minds and hearts.

What sets this book apart is Joseph Crown's unique offering of a collection of tools to structure, create, and track long-form hypnotic stories. In the realm of hypnotic storytelling, the term "episodic" embraces a diverse tapestry of meanings, each contributing to the artistry and potency of the hypnotic experience.

Think of this book as not for one specific type of person or group, but an incredible tool for self-discovery and a compass guiding you to discover others in a deeper and more fulfilling way. One could use the book going from cover to cover or even choose certain prompts as part of a “date night” conversation energizer with a romantic partner or an interesting way to get to know family or friends on a deeper level at a party or holiday celebration. I can imagine groups of people sitting around a dinner table answering questions and connecting in a way that many of us long to do.

Episodic storytelling in this context entails crafting tales composed of numerous distinct events or episodes, interconnected by an underlying theme or storyline. Like chapters in a novel, these captivating episodes guide the listener on a transformative odyssey, building suspense, intrigue, and emotional resonance along the way. Throughout this manual, readers will find a treasure trove of techniques, exercises, and practical guidance that will enable them to harness the art of hypnotic storytelling to create long-form transformative experiences. Whether you are a seasoned hypnotist or a budding storyteller, Joseph's approach will undoubtedly elevate your storytelling skills to new heights. The tracking tools that teach the storyteller how to bring hypnotic reference points from one story into another are pure genius!

As a Hypnotic Storyteller, I have been astounded by the transformative power of narratives on individuals, groups, and entire societies. Joseph Crown's unwavering commitment to empowering others through this art is nothing short of remarkable. Through this book, he bestows readers with the means to craft captivating long-form hypnotic stories, thus empowering them to shape lives and ignite positive change on a grand scale. His dedication to this endeavor is truly commendable. I am certain that the wisdom and techniques shared within these pages will resonate far and wide, leaving a lasting impact on all who embark on this journey of influence.

Jim Kellner

Introduction


Welcome to the fascinating world of Hypnotic Storytelling. My name is Joseph Crown, and I'm thrilled to be your guide on this transformative journey. With over 20 years of experience as a Hypno Dom, Guru, and Lifestyle Designer, I've witnessed the incredible potential of hypnotic storytelling in helping individuals achieve their goals and create lasting change. In this groundbreaking book, I bring this powerful technique to a wider audience for the very first time. What makes this book truly unique is its perspective as a world builder. Drawing from my extensive experience as a hypnotic dominant and a Guru, I have delved deep into the intricate dynamics of hierarchical relationships. As a lifestyle designer, I strive to craft stories that serve as guiding principles, enabling us to embark on profound journeys of self-discovery, explore the vastness of our world, and uncover hidden facets that lie beyond the reach of most individuals. So, what does this perspective bring to us?

It brings us a deeper understanding of hierarchical relationships, the power of storytelling, and the possibilities that exist within many types of relationships. It also brings us a unique perspective on the world and on the potential for growth and transformation that exists within all of us. It brings us the understanding that stories are not just entertainment; they are also powerful tools that can be used to transform our lives. It also brings us the hope that, by exploring the stories that we tell ourselves, we can discover new possibilities for ourselves and our world. By exploring our own inner worlds through stories, we can gain a deeper understanding of our own desires and needs. This can help us to make better choices about our lives and to create relationships that are more fulfilling. Unlock the ability to trace hypnotic effects across short stories, novels, series, and mesmerizing curated adventures. Designed to expand the limits of hypnotic storytelling, this guide equips you with the experience and tools to craft, track, transfer, merge, and mesh stories, experiential adventures, and the narratives we tell ourselves every day. Embrace the power of hypnotic storytelling and embark on a journey where imagination knows no bounds and influence weaves its magic through captivating tales.

This work has a collective focus on building medium to long-form epic stories showcases skills, techniques, and tools that someone who only uses short hypnotic stories will have no clue about. You can still create shorter hypnotic stories with this manual, but the tools are designed to allow you to stack them and establish a universe of experiences. In the context of long-form hypnotic storytelling, "episodic" refers to a narrative that is composed of multiple distinct events or episodes, meticulously woven together to create a larger tapestry of experiential tales. Each episode stands as a building block, intricately connected yet distinct, contributing to the unfolding story's grandeur.

Hypnotic episodic storytelling harnesses the power of multiple separate events to immerse the listener in a journey that transcends the boundaries of a single, linear storyline. The hypnotist, akin to an architect of consciousness, crafts these episodes with precision and artistry, engaging the audience's emotions, thoughts, and perceptions. As the narrative unfolds, it might venture into unexplored territories, each episode adding depth and nuance to the overall experience. The audience finds themselves drawn into a realm where each unfolding event presents new possibilities, making the journey all the more riveting. Moreover, hypnotic episodic storytelling demonstrates a certain ephemerality, resonating with the notion of limited duration to a single episode. In the transient world of hypnotic trance, each episode becomes a momentary gateway to altered states of consciousness, where profound shifts in perception and insight can occur in a fleeting instance.

Hypnosis, traditionally taught as a vocational skill, excels within the boundaries of a professional framework. However, its applications extend far beyond the conventional training, with individuals employing similar psychological principles and tools without explicitly labeling their work as "hypnotically based." In this book, you will gain profound insights into the ubiquitous nature of these techniques, observing their presence in marketing, sales, education, and everyday interactions. As you delve into its pages, you will not only recognize these methods in your daily life but also enhance your ability to present and communicate with others effectively. By fostering a collaborative learning environment, we encourage ethical awareness and consent, empowering our audience to navigate their context with mindfulness and expertise.

Whether you're a seasoned practitioner or completely new to the realm of hypnosis, this book is designed to equip you with the knowledge and skills to harness the captivating power of storytelling for influence and personal transformation. Throughout these pages, I will share with you the secrets, techniques, and insights I've gathered over the years, empowering you to become a master storyteller capable of guiding others on their own remarkable journeys. If you are completely new that is all the better! That means you will approach sections, topics, and hypnotic applications in ways that classically trained hypnotists will not. Sometimes that leads to exciting results and methods.

Drawing from my deep understanding of the human behavior and the dynamics of influence, I will reveal the art and science behind long-form hypnotic storytelling. Together, we will explore the intricate elements that make a story truly mesmerizing and delve into the psychology of how stories shape beliefs and behaviors. With practical guidance and immersive exercises, you will learn how to craft compelling narratives, utilize metaphors and symbolism, and weave language patterns that captivate the unconscious mind. But this book goes beyond theory and techniques. It delves into the ethical considerations of hypnotic storytelling, emphasizing the importance of consent, boundaries, and the responsible use of this powerful tool. Your growth and the well-being of those you interact with are at the forefront of our journey together.

The Constellation Frameworks here serve as dynamic lesson plans that enable you to delve into topics succinctly, providing a comprehensive understanding, facilitating collaboration, knowledge expansion, and practical application. In order to effectively convey ideas and foster mutual comprehension, certain sections may contain overlapping information, serving as introductions or explanations of concepts. Repetition plays a vital role as many concepts interrelate with one another. By engaging in this process, repetition ensures information retention and creates a reciprocal feedback loop, not only enhancing your individual understanding but also promoting effective collaboration with others. This intentional redundancy allows for easy reference to key concepts as you navigate the frameworks alongside your peers.

Prepare to embark on a path of personal and professional growth as you unlock the transformative potential of hypnotic storytelling. Whether you're a therapist, a salesperson, a performer, or simply someone seeking to make a positive impact, this book will empower you to engage hearts and minds, create meaningful connections, and inspire change. This work prominently describes these major themes and offers you an opportunity to explore them through your work.

The stories we tell ourselves are important. They shape our reality.

The stories we tell ourselves can be changed. But it takes time and effort.

The stories we tell ourselves are our own. We get to choose them.

In a world that constantly asks who you are, it's essential to seek silence amidst the noise and listen to where your heart and mind wander. As you hold tight to the harmony within yourself, you'll grow attuned to the world around you and find your true path.



"The words we use create our reality. The way we see the world is shaped by the language we use to describe it. When we become aware of the power of language, we can begin to change our reality. We can choose to use language that is empowering and uplifting, rather than language that is limiting and destructive. We can choose to use language that affirms our identity and our place in the world, rather than language that makes us feel small and insignificant. When we change the language we use, we change the world we live in. When we do that, we change the very nature of the stories used to live as ourselves."



Joseph W Crown 7/8/2023 Scrawled on a notepad at Wakin' Bakin on Banks Street and about to eat the most delicious biscuits and gravy you can imagine!

The Power of Stories: Unraveling the Psychological Impact of Storytelling


Stories have been an integral part of human civilization since time immemorial. From ancient myths to modern novels, storytelling has significantly shaped our culture, traditions, and understanding of the world. However, beyond their entertainment value, stories hold a profound psychological impact on individuals, shaping our emotions, beliefs, and behaviors. In this section, we'll explore the fascinating ways storytelling influences our minds, emotions, and even our sense of self.

Building Empathy and Emotional Connection

One of the most potent effects of storytelling is its ability to evoke empathy and emotional connection. As we immerse ourselves in a well-crafted narrative, we start identifying with the characters, experiencing their joys and sorrows as if they were our own. This process triggers the release of oxytocin, often referred to as the "love hormone," which enhances feelings of trust and bonding. By feeling a character's emotions, we become more empathetic towards others in real life, fostering understanding and compassion.

Emotional Resonance

Stories have an exceptional ability to evoke emotions within us. Whether it's laughter, sadness, fear, or joy, well-crafted narratives have the power to elicit profound emotional responses. By tapping into our emotions, stories create a unique connection between the storyteller and the audience, fostering a sense of shared experiences and forging strong emotional bonds.

Altering Perceptions and Beliefs

Stories have the power to challenge our preconceived notions and alter our beliefs. We encounter diverse perspectives, cultures, and ideologies through the lens of fictional worlds or real-life experiences. As we witness characters' growth and transformation, we may question our beliefs and values, leading to personal growth and expanded worldviews.

Meaning-Making and Sense of Purpose

Stories hold the key to understanding our lives and the world we dwell in. They delve into universal themes like love, loss, redemption, and the search for meaning. Through these narratives, we gain profound insights, wisdom, and guidance to navigate our unique paths. Stories empower us to discover purpose, unravel intricate emotions, and embrace life's uncertainties.

Engaging the Brain: The Neurological Effects

When we listen to a story, our brains become more active and engaged compared to merely receiving facts or information. Neuroscientists have found that storytelling activates multiple regions of the brain, including those responsible for language comprehension and sensory processing. Moreover, when listening to stories, our brains also synchronize with the storyteller, creating a sense of shared experience and connection.

Cognitive Development

Engaging with stories stimulates our cognitive processes and enhances our mental faculties. Stories challenge our minds, requiring us to follow plotlines, remember characters, and make connections. This cognitive workout strengthens our memory, creativity, problem-solving abilities, and critical thinking skills. Moreover, storytelling allows complex concepts and ideas to be conveyed in a more accessible and digestible manner, promoting intellectual growth and expanding our perspectives.

Relieving Stress and Anxiety

Engaging with stories can serve as a form of escapism, allowing us to momentarily distance ourselves from the stresses of daily life. Whether it's losing ourselves in a thrilling adventure or escaping to a serene fictional world, storytelling provides a valuable respite from the pressures of reality. This mental break can reduce stress levels, improve mood, and overall emotional well-being.

Shaping Our Sense of Identity

Stories play a crucial role in shaping our sense of identity. From childhood fairy tales that instill morals and values to personal anecdotes that define our self-concept, narratives create a framework for understanding who we are. Our life stories, constructed from our experiences and memories, provide us with a narrative continuity, a sense of purpose, and coherence in our lives.

Inspiring Action and Encouraging Change

Well-told stories can inspire us to take action and drive change. Historical accounts of courage and resilience, fictional tales of overcoming adversity, or narratives highlighting societal issues can mobilize individuals and communities toward a common cause. Storytelling has been a catalyst for social and political movements, igniting a desire for change and progress.

Cultural Preservation and Transmission

Stories are invaluable for preserving and transmitting cultural knowledge and heritage. They carry societies' collective wisdom, traditions, and values across generations, ensuring that important aspects of our cultural identity are not lost. Cultural narratives are passed down through stories, fostering a sense of belonging, shared history, and cultural cohesion.



The psychological impact of storytelling is undeniable and far-reaching. As we weave narratives into our lives, whether as creators or consumers, we experience a myriad of emotional, cognitive, and behavioral effects. From fostering empathy and connection to shaping our identities and inspiring change, stories possess an extraordinary ability to enrich our lives and the world around us. Next time you pick up a book, watch a movie, or share a personal experience, take a moment to recognize the power of storytelling and its profound impact on the human psyche. Embrace the stories that resonate with you, and perhaps even share your own narrative, for it is through storytelling that we find meaning, connection, and the shared essence of being human.





The Stories We Tell Ourselves


The stories we tell ourselves are not always true. But they can be very real to us. The stories we tell ourselves about ourselves have a powerful impact on our lives. They can shape our self-image, our beliefs about our capabilities, and our expectations for the future.

Negative self-talk can be incredibly harmful. When we constantly tell ourselves that we're not good enough, that we'll never succeed, or that we're not worthy of love, it becomes difficult to believe in ourselves and to take risks. We may shy away from challenges, avoid opportunities, and settle for less than we deserve.

Positive self-talk, on the other hand, can be incredibly empowering. When we focus on our strengths, remind ourselves of our accomplishments, and believe in our ability to achieve our goals, we're more likely to take action, persevere in the face of setbacks, and reach our full potential.

The stories we tell ourselves are not set in stone. We can choose to change them. If we're aware of the negative self-talk patterns we’ve developed, we can challenge them and replace them with more positive ones.

One way to do this is to keep a record of our self-talk. When you catch yourself thinking negative thoughts, write them down. Then, take a moment to challenge those thoughts. What evidence do you have to support them? What are some more positive ways to think about yourself? Another way to change our stories is to practice positive affirmations. Affirmations are positive statements that we repeat to ourselves on a regular basis. They can be about our character, our abilities, or our goals. When we repeat affirmations, we're essentially programming our subconscious minds with positive messages.

Over time, the stories we tell ourselves can profoundly impact our character and identity. If we tell ourselves positive stories, we're more likely to develop a positive self-image, believe in our ability to achieve our goals, and live a happy and fulfilling life.



The Stories We Pass On to Others

The stories we tell ourselves are not just for our own ears. We also pass them on to others, through our words and actions. When we're constantly negative about ourselves, it's likely that our friends and family will start to mirror that negativity. They may start to believe that we're not good enough, that we'll never succeed, or that we're not worthy of love. On the other hand, when we're positive about ourselves, our friends and family will start to mirror that positivity as well. They may start to believe in our abilities, encourage us to take risks, and support us in our goals.

The stories we pass on to others can have a powerful impact on their lives. We're setting them up for failure if we tell them negative stories. But if we tell them positive stories, we're giving them the tools they need to succeed.

The stories we tell ourselves and to others have a powerful impact on our lives. We can choose to tell ourselves negative stories that hold us back, or we can choose to tell ourselves positive stories that empower us to achieve our goals. The choice is ours.





We are here to learn Hypnotic Storytelling Works!


Stories are a powerful way to communicate ideas and emotions. They can transport us to new worlds, teach us about ourselves and others, and inspire us to take action. But did you know that stories can also influence our beliefs and behaviors?

That's right. The stories we read, watch, and tell ourselves can shape how we see the world and interact with it. For example, if we're constantly exposed to stories about heroes who overcome adversity, we're more likely to believe that we can do the same. Or, if we're constantly told stories about people who are victims of circumstance, we're more likely to believe that we're powerless to change our own lives.

The stories we tell ourselves can be just as influential as the stories we're told by others. Our self-talk is constantly shaping our beliefs and behaviors. We're more likely to feel discouraged and defeated when we tell ourselves negative stories. But when we tell ourselves positive stories, we're more likely to feel motivated and empowered.

How Hypnotic Storytelling Can Help Us Recognize and Work with Our Stories

Hypnotic storytelling is a powerful technique that can help us recognize and work with the stories shaping our lives. When we're in a hypnotic state, our minds are more open and receptive to suggestion. This makes it easier for us to access our subconscious thoughts and beliefs, and to begin to change them.

Hypnotic storytellers use various techniques to help us relax and enter a hypnotic state. They may use soothing music, repetitive words or phrases, or guided imagery. Once we're in a hypnotic state, the storyteller may begin to tell us a story designed to help us recognize and work with our own stories.

The stories that are told in hypnotic storytelling are often metaphorical or symbolic. This allows us to connect with the story on a deeper level and see our lives in a new light. The storyteller may also use techniques such as visualization and guided meditation to help us experience the story more immersive way. We will learn how to construct hypnotic text and the many techniques that are part of our language and communication that deliver stories hypnotically to others naturally.



Defining Hypnosis

In my work, I define hypnosis as a fascinating journey into a focused and goal-directed altered state of consciousness. This state can be induced by another person or self-induced, unlocking the doors to deeper communication with our subconscious mind. Hypnosis takes us to a realm of heightened awareness, where inhibitions fade away, the senses come alive, and peak responses are magnified. It is a gateway to exploring the realms of vivid imagination, where even the simplest tactile sensations can create realistic fantasy experiences, or where we can delve into intricate role-play scenes and narratives.

In this profound state of hypnosis, we tap into the power of our minds to experience emotional and expressive moments, where boundaries blur, and our senses become acutely attuned to the world around us. It is a journey of self-discovery and exploration, where the possibilities are as vast as the depths of our own subconscious. Through hypnosis, we unlock the potential to transcend the ordinary, embracing the extraordinary aspects of our desires and fantasies. Whether we seek thrilling adventures or intimate connections, the hypnotic state offers us a canvas upon which we can paint vibrant experiences and delve into the depths of our desires. It is a realm where our fantasies take shape and come to life, shaping our reality in ways that awaken our senses and bring us closer to our true selves.



Defining Trance

I define trance as an unguided and natural state of awareness. Trance is a state of focused, heightened awareness, where communication more easily happens between the conscious and subconscious mind. When we focus and flow, we feel ourselves transition into internal harmony. Each day we flow from one trance to another as we seek to achieve our goals and are present in the moments of our lives. The inclusion of guidance within the trance process promotes a concentrated and heightened state of hypnosis. When the story, exercise, or routine is designed to fractionate and/or develop a trance into hypnosis, this is called an inclusion trance.



The Enchanting World of Hypnotic Storytelling

Picture this: you're in a dimly lit room, surrounded by the soft glow of candlelight. Your guide, a skilled Hypno Dom or Story Telling Tist (hypnotist), or perhaps even you, gently leads a few people into this state of enchantment with a story you love. As the world around you fades away, your subconscious awakens, like a slumbering dragon roused from its deep sleep.

In this hypnotic trance, your subconscious mind is vibrant and eager to communicate and interact with the story it is receiving. It's as if a secret passage has been unlocked, revealing hidden treasures of memories, emotions, and desires. You find yourself immersed in the depths of opportunities, challenges, and details that your psyche engages with as if they are a secondary character in the tale holding a torch behind the characters. A vast ocean of possibilities is waiting to be explored, and you might wonder what you could do differently if a choice from a story was solely yours to make.

During this ethereal state, your conscious mind takes a step back, allowing your subconscious to take center stage. Like a grand conductor, your subconscious orchestrates the symphony of your thoughts, feelings, and beliefs. It becomes more active and communicative, speaking in whispers only the attentive soul can hear.

Meanwhile, like a curious child, your conscious mind maintains a concentration on the present moment, but it quickly gets overwhelmed with all the details. Distractions fade away, and the outside world becomes a distant hum. In this suspended reality, your imagination takes flight, and you become willing to participate in the unfolding story.

Now, let's share some hypnotic storytelling mechanisms so you can keep an eye out for this its magic:

Synchronized Rhythms: Just like a masterful melody, hypnotic storytelling creates a harmonious rhythm that syncs with the beats of your subconscious mind. The language can be soothing and persuasive, enticing your attention into a state of engaged receptivity.

Dialogue with the Unconscious: The art of hypnotic storytelling fosters a dialogue between your conscious and subconscious selves. It's a conversation between the captain and the navigator of your inner ship, guiding you towards new horizons of understanding.

Empowering Suggestions: Within the story lies a treasure trove of empowering suggestions. Like seeds planted in fertile soil, these suggestions take root in your subconscious, blossoming into positive change and growth.

Emotional Alchemy: Hypnotic storytelling elicits a kaleidoscope of emotions, transcending the boundaries of rationality. Emotions become the symphony's keynotes, amplifying the narrative's impact.

Personalization and Connection: The artful storyteller tailors the tale to resonate with your unique experiences and aspirations. This personalization creates a deep connection, fostering trust and openness to transformation.
